down that I missed something and one of my clients got hurt because of me, like they are just not telling me yet.\n- key: S020\n  name: Hallucinations\n  description: Perception-like experiences that occur without an external stimulus and are as vivid and clear as normal perception.\n  manifestation: When I'm alone in the car or in a quiet client's house, I hear a voice clear as somebody standing I don't want to make it sound worse than it is. I'm just having a little trouble sleeping, that's all. I haven't had any of that paralysis stuff. Does that... does that help rule things out for you?\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself having trouble turning off your thoughts or feeling worried when you're trying to fall asleep?\n\nPatient: Um, well... I guess I do think a lot, but I wouldn't say I'm, like, overly worried or anything. It's just normal stuff, you know? Like, did I do everything right at work, or... did I forget to lock the door. Just little things. \n\nI definitely don't want to sound like I'm complaining or like I can't handle my own thoughts. Everyone gets a little wound up sometimes, right? I just try to focus on being a good worker and getting through the night. I hope that makes sense? I'm really trying to give you the right information.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself feeling very sleepy or tired during the day, even after you’ve had some sleep?\n\nPatient: Um, no, not really. I mean, maybe just a normal amount? Since I work as a personal care aide, I'm on my feet helping people a lot, so... so I might get a little worn out by the afternoon. I hope I'm still being helpful with all this...
